=== idx: 957 ===

LLM supporting:  transcription - coupled repair ( tcr ) primarily targets the non - template ( or sense ) strand of dna. this is because tcr is initiated in response to damage that is encountered by the rna polymerase during transcription, and the non - template strand is the one being transcribed. the process involves the removal of the transcribed rna polymerase and the recruitment of the nucleotide excision repair ( ner ) machinery, which then excises the damaged segment of the non - template dna strand. this allows for the correction of the dna lesion and the resumption of transcription. however, the exact mechanisms and the specific types of dna damage that tcr can handle, such as oxidative lesions, are still under investigation.
